  is robust. what is the option to add the necessary support to export this     
  shader and do it robustly?                                                    

● Robust plan for Surface.shadergraph

  The "right" way to handle keywords is compile-time #ifdef variants, since your
   engine supports it. Here's a complete plan that's robust to all six missing
  pieces, ordered by structural impact:

  A. Keyword infrastructure (the big architectural piece)

  Parse keywords — SgGraph.Keywords populated from m_Keywords. Each keyword
  carries { ReferenceName, Type (Bool/Enum), Entries (for Enum), DefaultValue }.

  New IR node — SgConditionalStatement:
  <type> _nN;
  #ifdef _BLEND_NORMALS_ON
  {
      <on-branch local statements>
      _nN = <on-rhs>;
  }
  #else
  {
      <off-branch local statements>
      _nN = <off-rhs>;
  }
  #endif
  The temp's declaration lives outside the #ifdef (so it's in scope after); each
   branch is its own block scope so branch-local temps don't leak. For enum
  keywords, chain #elif defined(...) per entry, with the default-entry as #else.

  KeywordNodeHandler — lowers each variant input into a fresh nested LowerCtx,
  captures its statements + the resulting SgRhs, builds an
  SgConditionalStatement. The result is one named temp the consumer can
  reference like any other.

  Optimizer walks — extend RenameRefs, SubstituteOnce, and DCE to traverse
  conditional branches. Inline-once-used should refuse to inline into a
  conditional (different scope = unsafe). Constant folding works inside each
  branch independently. ~30 LOC across the four switch statements we already
  have.

  Engine handshake — Emit a comment header listing the keywords used:
  // @keyword _BLEND_NORMALS Boolean default=Off
  // @keyword _SURFACE_MODE Enum entries=Opaque,Cutout default=Opaque
  Your engine's shader compiler reads this and generates the variant matrix.
  Materials toggle them via the engine's existing material system.

  B. Six missing nodes

  Node: KeywordNode
  Approach: Custom handler (above)
  LOC: ~50
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Node: ContrastNode
  Approach: Manual Pattern(...) line — body is (In - 0.2176) * Contrast + 0.2176

    (the pow(0.5, 2.2) constant precomputed)
  LOC: 1
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Node: NormalBlendNode
  Approach: Manual Pattern(...) — normalize(vec3(A.rg + B.rg, A.b * B.b))
    (default mode)
  LOC: 1
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Node: NormalStrengthNode
  Approach: Manual Pattern(...) — vec3(In.rg * Strength, mix(1.0, In.b, 
    clamp(Strength, 0.0, 1.0)))
  LOC: 1
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Node: TilingAndOffsetNode
  Approach: Verify it auto-generated (body is UV * Tiling + Offset); if not,
    manual pattern
  LOC: 0–1
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Node: TransformNode
  Approach: Custom handler — reads m_ConversionType, m_FromSpace, m_ToSpace. For

    our usage scope: emit (v.model * vec4(in, 0.0)).xyz for Object→World on
    vectors, (transpose(v.model) * vec4(in, 0.0)).xyz for World→Object, identity

    for same-space. Throw on unsupported transforms.
  LOC: ~40

  C. Lit-template completeness

  Surface is a Lit graph with 8+ fragment blocks. Today we only lower BaseColor 
  / Metallic / Smoothness. For correctness need:

  - NormalTS (tangent-space normal, vec3, default vec3(0, 0, 1))
  - Emission (vec3, default vec3(0))
  - Occlusion (float, default 1.0)

  Plumb them through to the lit template's PBR call. The engine's
  standard_static.hz.glsl already shows the structure (apply_normal_map, orm
  reads). We'd:
  1. Lower these three blocks as additional Assigns.
  2. Update the LIT_TEMPLATE to reference mat_normal_ts, mat_emission,
  mat_occlusion and feed them into the lighting calls.

  Order of attack

  1. The 3 simple manual patterns + TilingAndOffsetNode check (~5 min) — gets
  the easy ones out of the way.
  2. TransformNode handler (~20 min) — also low-risk.
  3. Lit-template extra blocks (~30 min) — orthogonal to keywords, makes the lit
   path complete.
  4. Keyword infrastructure (~2–3 hrs) — the big one. Done in this order:
    - a. Parser (keywords → SgGraph.Keywords)
    - b. SgConditionalStatement IR + Format()
    - c. Optimizer pass updates
    - d. KeywordNodeHandler (lower each branch in nested ctx)
    - e. Header comment emission
  5. Re-export Surface, debug — likely 30 min of fixing edge cases.

  Total: ~4 hours of focused work, ~300 LOC.

  Robustness checks I'd verify

  - A keyword used in a subgraph: keyword references should propagate up so the
  parent shader's header lists them.
  - A keyword feeding into another keyword (nested conditionals): branch
  lowering should recurse cleanly.
  - Same temp referenced from multiple branches: must be hoisted to the outer
  scope (the existing optimizer cache already does this, since both branches go
  through the same Output() cache; just need to NOT scope branch-local temps
  inside { } if they were emitted before the conditional).
  - Inline-once-used must NOT inline a temp into a conditional branch — else the
   temp's RHS could reference identifiers that aren't in the branch's scope. Add
   an "is inside conditional" guard.

  Want me to proceed with this plan? If yes, I'd suggest doing steps 1–3 first
  (low-risk, makes the graph closer to exporting), then commit, then tackle the
  keyword piece as its own focused change.

      599 +    // TransformNode: coordinate-space conversion. m_Conversio
          +n.{from,to}                                                   
      600 +    // identifies the spaces (0=Object, 1=View, 2=World, 3=Tan
          +gent,                                                         
      601 +    // 4=AbsoluteWorld, 5=Screen); m_ConversionType is 0=Posit
          +ion, 1=Direction,                                             
      602 +    // 2=Normal. We support the conversions we've actually hit
          +; others throw                                                
      603 +    // until they come up so we add them deliberately.        
      604 +    class TransformNodeHandler : NodeHandler                  
      605 +    {                                                         
      606 +        public override SgValue Lower(SgNode node, int outSlot
          +Num, LowerCtx ctx)                                            
      607 +        {                                                     
      608 +            int from = ReadInt(node, "m_Conversion", "from"); 
      609 +            int to   = ReadInt(node, "m_Conversion", "to");   
      610 +            int kind = 0;                                     
      611 +            if (node.Raw.TryGetValue("m_ConversionType", out v
          +ar ct) && ct is double d) kind = (int)d;                      
      612 +                                                              
      613 +            var input = ctx.InputByName(node, "In");          
      614 +                                                              
      615 +            if (from == to)                                   
      616 +                return new SgValue { Rhs = input.Rhs, Type = S
          +gType.Vec3, IsTemp = false };                                 
      617 +                                                              
      618 +            // Tangent → World direction/normal: build worldDi
          +r = T*x + B*y + N*z                                           
      619 +            // where B is the per-vertex-handed cross product.
          + Engine's @fs                                                 
      620 +            // exposes v_tangent, v_normal, v_bitangent_sign f
          +or this exact

      628 +                    input.Rhs);

      630 +            }                                                 
      631 +                                                              
      632 +            // Object → World: model matrix; w-component depen
          +ds on kind                                                    
      633 +            // (1.0 for positions, 0.0 for directions/normals)
          +.                                                             
      634 +            if (from == 0 && to == 2)                         
      635 +            {                                                 
      636 +                string w = (kind == 0) ? "1.0" : "0.0";       
      637 +                var rhs = Sg.RawTemplate("(v.model * vec4({0},
          + " + w + ")).xyz", input.Rhs);                                
      638 +                return ctx.EmitTemp(SgType.Vec3, rhs);        
      639 +            }                                                 
      640 +                                                              
      641 +            throw new System.NotImplementedException(         
      642 +                $"TransformNode space {from}->{to} (kind={kind
          +}) not supported");                                           
      643 +        }

● Now the KeywordNodeHandler:

● Update(D:/dev/unity/UnityToHZ/Assets/Editor/HZExporter/ShaderGraph/SgLowering.
        cs)
  ⎿  Added 116 lines
      610          }
      611      }
      612  
      613 +    // KeywordNode: a `#ifdef`-driven compile-time branch on a
          + shader keyword.                                              
      614 +    // For each variant input on the node, we lower the chain 
          +feeding it in                                                 
      615 +    // a fresh nested LowerCtx whose temps live inside the bra
          +nch's `{ }`                                                   
      616 +    // block (separate prefix prevents collisions). The result
          + is an                                                        
      617 +    // SgConditionalStatement that declares one outer-scope te
          +mp and assigns                                                
      618 +    // it inside each `#if defined(...)` branch.              
      619 +    class KeywordNodeHandler : NodeHandler                    
      620 +    {                                                         
      621 +        public override SgValue Lower(SgNode node, int outSlot
          +Num, LowerCtx ctx)                                            
      622 +        {                                                     
      623 +            if (!node.Raw.TryGetValue("m_Keyword", out var k) 
          +|| !(k is Dictionary<string, object> kd)                      
      624 +                || !kd.TryGetValue("m_Id", out var kid) || !(k
          +id is string keywordId))                                      
      625 +                throw new System.Exception("KeywordNode missin
          +g m_Keyword reference");                                      
      626 +            if (!ctx.Graph.KeywordsById.TryGetValue(keywordId,
          + out var keyword))                                            
      627 +                throw new System.Exception($"KeywordNode refer
          +ences unknown keyword '{keywordId}'");                        
      628 +                                                              
      629 +            ctx.UsedKeywords.Add(keyword.Id);                 
      630 +                                                              
      631 +            var branches = new List<SgConditionalBranch>();   
      632 +            SgType outType = SgType.Unknown;                  
      633 +                                                              
      634 +            if (keyword.IsBoolean)                            
      635 +            {                                                 
      636 +                // Slot layout: 0=Out, 1=On, 2=Off. Default-va
          +lue 0=Off → the                                               
      637 +                // Off branch is the #else; On branch is gated
          + by `<REF>_ON`.                                               
      638 +                int onSlot = node.SlotByName.TryGetValue("On",
          + out var os) ? os : 1;                                        
      639 +                int offSlot = node.SlotByName.TryGetValue("Off
          +", out var fs) ? fs : 2;                                      
      640 +                var on  = LowerBranch(node, onSlot,  $"{keywor
          +d.ReferenceName}_ON", ctx);                                   
      641 +                var off = LowerBranch(node, offSlot, null,    
          +                      ctx);                                   
      642 +                branches.Add(on);                             
      643 +                branches.Add(off);                            
      644 +                outType = on.Rhs is SgRhsRef onRef ? onRef.Typ
          +e :                                                           
      645 +                          (off.Rhs is SgRhsRef offRef ? offRef
          +.Type : SgType.Vec4);                                         
      646 +            }                                                 
      647 +            else                                              
      648 +            {                                                 
      649 +                // Enum: one branch per entry. Default entry b
          +ecomes the #else                                              
      650 +                // (no define guard). Other entries gated by `
          +<REF>_<ENTRY>`.                                               
      651 +                int defaultIdx = System.Math.Max(0, System.Mat
          +h.Min(keyword.DefaultValue, keyword.Entries.Count - 1));      
      652 +                for (int i = 0; i < keyword.Entries.Count; i++
          +)                                                             
      653 +                {                                             
      654 +                    var entry = keyword.Entries[i];           
      655 +                    if (!node.SlotByName.TryGetValue(entry.Dis
          +playName, out var slot))                                      
      656 +                        throw new System.Exception(           
      657 +                            $"KeywordNode '{keyword.Name}' has
          + no slot for entry '{entry.DisplayName}'");                   
      658 +                    string define = i == defaultIdx ? null : $
          +"{keyword.ReferenceName}_{entry.ReferenceName}";              
      659 +                    branches.Add(LowerBranch(node, slot, defin
          +e, ctx));                                                     
      660 +                }                                             
      661 +                // Reorder so the #else (default) branch comes
          + last.                                                        
      662 +                int elseIdx = branches.FindIndex(b => b.Define
          + == null);                                                    
      663 +                if (elseIdx >= 0 && elseIdx != branches.Count 
          +- 1)                                                          
      664 +                {                                             
      665 +                    var def = branches[elseIdx];              
      666 +                    branches.RemoveAt(elseIdx);               
      667 +                    branches.Add(def);                        
      668 +                }                                             
      669 +                if (branches.Count > 0 && branches[0].Rhs is S
          +gRhsRef r0) outType = r0.Type;                                
      670 +                else outType = SgType.Vec4;                   
      671 +            }                                                 
      672 +                                                              
      673 +            // Emit `<type> _nN;` then the conditional block. 
          +Reuse the                                                     
      674 +            // standard temp counter so the result is renumber
          +able like any                                                 
      675 +            // other outer temp.                              
      676 +            string name = ctx.TempPrefix + (ReadTempCounter(ct
          +x));                                                          
      677 +            BumpTempCounter(ctx);                             
      678 +            ctx.Statements.Add(new SgStatement                
      679 +            {                                                 
      680 +                Name = name,                                  
      681 +                Type = outType,                               
      682 +                Branches = branches,                          
      683 +            });                                               
      684 +            return SgValue.Temp(name, outType);               
      685 +        }                                                     
      686 +                                                              
      687 +        SgConditionalBranch LowerBranch(SgNode node, int slotN
          +um, string define, LowerCtx parentCtx)                        
      688 +        {                                                     
      689 +            int idx = parentCtx.NextBranchIndex();            
      690 +            var branchCtx = new LowerCtx(parentCtx.Graph)     
      691 +            {                                                 
      692 +                IsVertexStage = parentCtx.IsVertexStage,      
      693 +                IsSubGraph = parentCtx.IsSubGraph,            
      694 +                TempPrefix = $"_b{idx}_n",                    
      695 +            };                                                
      696 +            // Lower the value flowing into the branch's input
          + slot. This may                                               
      697 +            // recursively emit temps into branchCtx.Statement
          +s (branch-local).                                             
      698 +            var v = branchCtx.Input(node.Id, slotNum);        
      699 +            // Promote any subgraph functions surfaced inside 
          +the branch.                                                   
      700 +            parentCtx.Functions.AddRange(branchCtx.Functions);
      701 +            // Propagate keyword usage too.                   
      702 +            foreach (var kw in branchCtx.UsedKeywords) parentC
          +tx.UsedKeywords.Add(kw);                                      
      703 +            return new SgConditionalBranch                    
      704 +            {                                                 
      705 +                Define = define,                              
      706 +                Body = branchCtx.Statements,                  
      707 +                Rhs = v.Rhs,                                  
      708 +            };                                                
      709 +        }                                                     
      710 +                                                              
      711 +        // Reflection-free access to LowerCtx._tempCounter (wh
          +ich is private).                                              
      712 +        // Reading it via the public-ish behaviour of EmitTemp
          + would also bump                                              
      713 +        // it, so we inline the logic. These two helpers are g
          +ross but isolate                                              
      714 +        // the friendliness break to the smallest possible sur
          +face.                                                         
      715 +        static int ReadTempCounter(LowerCtx ctx)              
      716 +        {                                                     
      717 +            // Keep counter in sync by emitting a placeholder 
          +temp via the                                                  
      718 +            // public path then immediately removing it — keep
          +s the counter                                                 
      719 +            // monotonic without exposing a setter.           
      720 +            int n = ctx.Statements.Count;                     
      721 +            ctx.EmitTemp(SgType.Float, Sg.Lit(0.0));          
      722 +            int counter = int.Parse(ctx.Statements[n].Name.Sub
          +string(ctx.TempPrefix.Length));                               
      723 +            ctx.Statements.RemoveAt(n);                       
      724 +            return counter;                                   
      725 +        }                                                     
      726 +        static void BumpTempCounter(LowerCtx ctx) { /* counter
          + already advanced by ReadTempCounter */ }                     
      727 +    }                                                         
      728 +                                                              
      729      // TransformNode: coordinate-space conversion. m_Conversio
           n.{from,to}
      730      // identifies the spaces (0=Object, 1=View, 2=World, 3=Tan
           gent,
      731      // 4=AbsoluteWorld, 5=Screen); m_ConversionType is 0=Posit
           ion, 1=Direction,

  default=VERTEX_POSITION
  // @keyword _ENABLE_COVERAGE Boolean default=Off
  // @keyword _BLEND_NORMALS Boolean default=Off

  Conditional output (notice: nested #if/#else, branch-local _b<idx>_n<i> temps 
  in { } scopes, outer-scope assignment to _b4_n3):
  #if defined(_BLEND_NORMALS_ON)
  {
      vec2 _b0_n0 = (vec2(0.0, 0.0) * mat.base_map_scale + vec2(0.0, 0.0));
      vec4 _b0_n1 = texture_sample(normal_map, mat.normal_map, _b0_n0);
      vec3 _b0_n2 = vec3(_b0_n1.rg * mat.normal_strength, mix(1.0, _b0_n1.b,
  clamp(mat.normal_strength, 0.0, 1.0)));
      vec2 _b0_n3 = (vec2(0.0, 0.0) * mat.coverage_map_scale + vec2(0.0, 0.0));
      vec4 _b0_n4 = texture_sample(coverage_normal_map, mat.coverage_normal_map,
   _b0_n3);
      vec3 _b0_n5 = vec3(_b0_n4.rg * mat.coverage_normal_strength, mix(1.0,
  _b0_n4.b, clamp(mat.coverage_normal_strength, 0.0, 1.0)));
      vec3 _b0_n6 = normalize(vec3(_b0_n2.rg + _b0_n5.rg, _b0_n2.b * _b0_n5.b));
      _b4_n3 = _b0_n6;
  }
  #else
  {
      vec2 _b1_n0 = (vec2(0.0, 0.0) * mat.coverage_map_scale + vec2(0.0, 0.0));
      vec4 _b1_n1 = texture_sample(coverage_normal_map, mat.coverage_normal_map,
   _b1_n0);
      vec3 _b1_n2 = vec3(_b1_n1.rg * mat.coverage_normal_strength, mix(1.0,
  _b1_n1.b, clamp(mat.coverage_normal_strength, 0.0, 1.0)));
      _b4_n3 = _b1_n2;
  }
  #endif

  Each branch:
  - Wrapped in { } so its locals (_b0_n*) don't leak.
  - Branch-local prefix (_b0_, _b1_) prevents collisions across branches.
  - Final value assigned to the outer-scope temp (_b4_n3) declared just before
  the #if.
  - All correctly nested (the _BLEND_NORMALS_ON block is itself inside an outer
  _ENABLE_COVERAGE_ON block).

  NormalBlendNode and NormalStrengthNode (both manually-registered patterns we
  added) folded cleanly into the inner branches.

❯ sorry, is there any todo / incomplete things you didn't cover? e.g            
  PositionNode? explain, and let me know if there is anything else              

  Searched for 1 pattern (ctrl+o to expand)

● Audit of incomplete items, ranked by likelihood you'll hit them:

  Hit by the Surface graph specifically

  1. PositionNode in fragment stage with non-World space — falls back to
  v_world_pos with a // TODO comment in the output. Surface uses Object space
  somewhere; the result is probably visually wrong (object-position-based
  effects like dust accumulation or local-coord noise will instead get
  world-space input). To fix: add a v_object_pos varying to the lit template
  (vec3 passed through from v.pos) and route Object-space PositionNode in @fs to
   it. ~10 LOC.

  2. Per-branch optimizer pass — KeywordNode branches are lowered but not run
  through SgOptimize.Run (no DCE / peephole / inline-once-used inside a branch).
   You'll see verbose temps in branches that won't get collapsed. The output is
  correct but uglier than necessary. To fix: in KeywordNodeHandler.LowerBranch,
  after lowering call SgOptimize.Run(branchCtx.Statements, 
  [wrapper-around-Rhs]). ~10 LOC.

  Probably-fine for Surface, hits other graphs

  3. TransformNode — only Tangent→World and Object→World for direction/normal.
  View, Screen, AbsoluteWorld, World→Object, etc. throw. Surface only uses
  Tangent→World direction so it's fine here, but any other graph using a
  different conversion will fail.

  4. NormalVectorNode — only World space (v_normal). Object/Tangent/View throw.
  Surface doesn't hit this; lofi-style shaders typically don't either.

  5. UV channel >1 in UVNode throws. Engine only has v_uv and v_uv1, so this is
  also an engine-side limitation.

  6. TimeNode slots 3/4 (Delta / SmoothDelta) throw — engine doesn't track
  per-frame delta. Most shader graphs don't use those.

  7. SamplerState input on SampleTexture2D throws if wired. Engine uses an
  implicit default sampler so users typically leave it unconnected.

  8. Subgraph Texture2D inputs throw. Our test subgraphs only use scalar/vec
  inputs. If a subgraph parameterizes a texture, would need to thread the
  texture handle through as a function param.

  Unhandled Unity nodes (41 in tools/sg_nodes_skipped.txt)

  These will throw no handler registered for 'XxxNode' if any graph uses them:

  BlackbodyNode, CheckerboardNode, ColorMaskNode, CombineNode, DDXNode,
  DDXYNode,
  DDYNode, DitherNode, EllipseNode, EyeIndexNode, FogNode, HueNode,
  NoiseSineWaveNode, NormalReconstructZNode, PolarCoordinatesNode, PolygonNode,
  RadialShearNode, RandomRangeNode, RectangleNode, ReplaceColorNode,
  RotateAboutAxisNode, RotateNode, RoundedPolygonNode

  (plus a few more in the file). Most are multi-statement HLSL bodies that would
   need hand-written patterns or custom handlers when they come up. Each is ≤1
  hour.

  Engine-side concerns (not transpiler bugs)

  9. Skinning / blendshape vertex pre-pass — hand-written character shaders
  (lofi_basic.hz.glsl, lofi_character.hz.glsl) have apply_blendshapes(...) and
  apply_skinning(...) calls in @vs main(). We don't generate these. Whether a
  mesh needs them depends on the mesh asset, not the graph — needs an
  out-of-band signal (a per-graph metadata or material flag).

  10. Texture default annotation (@white / @black / @normal) — we always emit
  @texture2D (the basic annotation). The engine's hand-written shaders use
  @white/@black to declare fallback-texture defaults. Source graph's
  m_DefaultType is always 0 in our test data; not clear it's reliably set by
  users. Inferring "this is a normal map" from the graph might be possible (if
  the texture is consumed by SampleTexture2D type=Normal Map or via
  TransformNode tangent→world), but I haven't built that.

  11. Lit NormalTS is always run through apply_normal_map — which assumes
  tangent-space input. If a user's graph manually does Tangent→World (like
  Surface seems to via TransformNode), then writes the world-space result to
  NormalTS, we'd double-transform. Probably means Surface's normal output is
  wrong. The engine has @material block options but I don't know the right way
  to short-circuit apply_normal_map. Worth checking the actual Surface output
  line that builds N — and verifying against what the user expects.

  Robustness gaps in the IR

  12. SgRhsRawTemplate doesn't render branches — if you put a RawTemplate whose
  substituted operands include conditional refs from inside a branch body, it
  could emit references to branch-local temps from outside the branch. Not
  currently triggered by any test, but worth a guard.

  13. Inline-once-used through subgraph function calls — currently treats them
  as opaque (good), but if a subgraph returns a constant (like vec4(0,0,0,0)),
  we don't fold that across the call boundary. The GPU compiler will, so it's a
  readability issue not correctness.

  14. Optimizer doesn't constant-fold across keyword branches — if both #if and
  #else paths produce the same constant, we still emit both branches. Fine for
  correctness; pretty rare in practice.

  Bottom-line recommendation

  For getting Surface running on the engine I'd address:

  1. Visually verify the output compiles and runs. The v_world_pos /* TODO */ on
   Object-space PositionNode and the apply_normal_map always-on are the two
  specific things I'd watch.
  2. Add the per-branch optimizer pass (~10 LOC) — cleans up the 270-line output
   significantly.
  3. Add v_object_pos varying if the Surface visual confirms Object-space
  position is needed.

  Everything else is "later when a graph hits it."

  Want me to do (2) and the v_object_pos plumbing right now? Both are short.
